Result: 大量实验和感知研究表明，SiGnature在保持流畅自然共语手势生成和说话者独特特征的同时，提供了优越的语义运动控制，优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: SiGnature实现了语义控制与风格保真度的调和，支持即插即用的任意运动集成，无需重新训练，且避免了剪贴方法常见的"弗兰肯斯坦"伪影。

Abstract: While recent advances in co-speech gesture generation have achieved impressive rhythmic synchronization, synthesizing gestures that are both semantically meaningful and faithful to a speaker's unique non-verbal style remains an open challenge. Semantic gestures, such as iconic shapes or deictic pointing, are statistically sparse, making them difficult to learn effectively within standard generative models. We present SiGnature, a framework for Stylized and Semantic Gesture generation that reconciles precise semantic control with high-fidelity style preservation. Unlike prevalent methods that rely on entangled latent representations, SiGnature operates in an explicit joint-rotation space. This design enables our core contribution, Joint Motion Integration (JMI), a training-free inference mechanism capable of injecting any external motion sequence, particularly in-the-wild semantic gestures, directly into the diffusion process. JMI automatically identifies the specific ``active joints'' conveying a semantic action and injects them into the generation, while relying on the diffusion backbone to synthesize the remaining body dynamics, including posture and flow, in accordance with the pre-learned style of the target speaker. This allows for the plug-and-play integration of arbitrary motions, including complex semantic gestures, without retraining or introducing the ``Frankenstein'' artifacts typical of cut-and-paste methods. Extensive experiments and perceptual studies demonstrate that SiGnature offers superior semantic motion control while maintaining smooth and natural co-speech gesture generation and preserving the distinct characteristics of the speaker, thereby outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [148] [High-Fidelity 4D Hand-Object Capture via Multi-View Spatiotemporal Tracking and Physics-Aware Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15908)
*Bo Peng,Xu Chen,Yi Gu,Hidenobu Matsuki,Mingsong Dou,Jingjing Shen,Deying Kong,Juyong Zhang,Zhengyang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模板和标记的多视角视频4D手物交互重建系统，通过跨视角几何-时态Transformer初始化和物理感知高斯优化，实现鲁棒且无伪影的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D手物交互重建方法依赖预扫描物体模板和物理标记，且对初始手物姿态估计高度敏感，在严重遮挡场景下鲁棒性差。

Method: (1) 多视角前馈Transformer模型，聚合跨视角几何和时序线索，提供可靠的度量一致初始化；(2) 基于高斯的手物物理感知优化框架，集成四面体约束、碰撞精修和外观分解。

Result: 在公开基准和内部数据集上实现了高度鲁棒、无伪影的重建，为自动化4D资产生成提供高效基础。

Conclusion: 所提系统无需模板和标记即可实现鲁棒精确的4D手物交互重建，有效克服了遮挡和初始姿态敏感性问题。

Abstract: The growing demand for high-fidelity 4D hand-object interaction (HOI) data in embodied AI and spatial computing is currently bottlenecked by the reliance on pre-scanned object templates and physical markers. While recent methods have demonstrated promising results in reconstructing 4D hand-object interaction from videos, they are highly sensitive to initial estimates of hand and object poses. Yet, estimating these poses from images is challenging, in particular under severe occlusion which is inherent in hand-object interaction scenarios. We propose a novel system for the robust and accurate reconstruction of hands and objects from synchronized and calibrated multi-view videos without requiring any templates or markers. Our system consists of two main components with key innovations: (1) a multi-view feed-forward transformer model that aggregates cross-view geometry and temporal cues to provide a reliable, metric-consistent initialization for both poses and dense object geometry, and (2) a hand-object physics-aware Gaussian-based optimization framework to refine the initial estimates, integrating tetrahedral constraints, collision refinement, and appearance decomposition to produce physically plausible and visually accurate reconstruction. Validated on public benchmarks and an extensive internal dataset, our pipeline achieves highly robust, artifact-free reconstruction, providing an efficient foundation for automated 4D asset generation. Our project page are available at https://zyshen021.github.io/HOSTPG/.

</details>


### [149] [OmniOPSD: Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Affective Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15920)
*Zebang Cheng,Shuimu Chen,Boxue Yang,Yuanshen Guan,Jingyi Chen,Zheng Lian,Xiaojiang Peng,Fei Ma,LaiZhong Cui,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniOPSD框架，通过前沿模型生成的推理依据作为教师特权证据，在训练时提供密集令牌级监督，解决多模态大语言模型在复杂推理任务中的奖励稀疏问题，无需标注数据或闭源模型即可实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂推理任务中面临严重的奖励稀疏问题，尤其是在涉及状态、情感、意图和行为的人机交互场景中，异构多模态信号和主观人为因素导致高质量思维链标注昂贵且难以获取；直接使用专家标注的标签进行监督微调可能鼓励捷径学习，且对安全关键的人机交互缺乏透明度。

Method: 提出OmniOPSD（Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation）框架，利用前沿模型生成的推理依据作为教师侧特权证据而非学生模仿目标；仅在训练时作为局部教师的特权证据上下文，学生从原始多模态输入采样自身轨迹，由依据特权教师对相同令牌进行评分并提供密集令牌级监督。

Result: 在MER-UniBench上达到SOTA性能，平均得分84.19；消融实验进一步支持了依据特权教师引导的价值。

Conclusion: OmniOPSD使学生在其自身轨迹分布上学习而不直接模仿前沿模型完成，推理时无需标签、依据、思维链标注或闭源模型访问，有效解决了奖励稀疏问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is often hindered by severe reward sparsity in complex reasoning tasks. This challenge is particularly pronounced in human-centered scenarios involving states, emotions, intentions, and behaviors, where heterogeneous multimodal signals and subjective human factors make high-quality chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations expensive and difficult to obtain. Although many multimodal datasets provide expert-annotated ground-truth labels, directly using these labels for supervised fine-tuning may encourage shortcut learning in multimodal perception and provides limited transparency for safety-critical human--AI interaction. To address these limitations, we propose OmniOPSD, a Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation framework that uses frontier-generated rationales as teacher-side privileged evidence rather than student imitation targets. OmniOPSD uses frontier-generated evidence-aware rationales only as training-time privileged evidence context for a local teacher. The student samples its own rollout from the original multimodal input, while the rationale-privileged teacher scores the same tokens and provides dense token-level supervision. Thus, the student learns on its own trajectory distribution without directly imitating frontier-model completions, and inference requires no labels, rationales, CoT annotations, or closed-source model access. Experiments on MER-UniBench show that OmniOPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average score of $84.19$, and ablations further support the value of rationale-privileged teacher guidance.

</details>


### [150] [TurboGS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting via Error-Guided Sparse Pixel Sampling and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15924)
*Zheng Dong,Daifei Qiu,Pinxuan Dai,Ke Xu,Jiamin Xu,Lili He,Rynson W. H. Lau,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboGS提出了一种基于误差引导的训练框架，通过稀疏像素采样、结构感知损失、误差驱动的高斯密度控制和混合优化器，在单个RTX 5090上100秒内实现与原始3DGS相当或更优的渲染质量，训练速度提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级应用需要快速优化3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）以实现高保真新视角渲染，但现有加速方法在冗余像素上仍存在大量计算，同时牺牲了细节质量。

Method: (1) 基于多视图重建误差的tile级稀疏像素采样，优先处理困难区域、跳过已良好重建区域；(2) tile级结构感知损失与稀疏归一化互相关，提供稀疏但有效的监督；(3) 误差驱动的高斯密度控制，动态分配模型容量并移除冗余基元；(4) 结合Hessian更新与Adam动量阻尼的混合优化器，稳定收敛。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，TurboGS在单个RTX 5090上100秒内达到与原始3DGS相当或更优的渲染质量，训练速度提升高达10倍。

Conclusion: TurboGS通过聚焦于感知信息丰富的像素来加速3DGS优化，在保持高保真渲染的同时显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Consumer-level applications require fast optimization of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with high-fidelity novel view rendering. However, existing 3DGS acceleration approaches still incur substantial computation on redundant pixels while sacrificing fine details. In this paper, we present TurboGS, an error-guided training framework that accelerates 3DGS by concentrating optimization on perceptually informative pixels. TurboGS is built upon four core components: (1) a tile-wise sparse pixel sampling, which, driven by multi-view reconstruction errors during training, prioritizes challenging regions and skips well-reconstructed ones to avoid redundant gradient computation; (2) a tile-wise structure-aware loss with sparse Normalized Cross-Correlation, which provides sparse yet effective supervision to preserve fine details and stabilize training; (3) an error-driven Gaussian density control strategy, which dynamically allocates model capacity and removes redundant primitives; and (4) a tailored hybrid optimizer that couples Hessian-informed updates with Adam moment damping to stabilize and improve convergence under sparse supervision. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that TurboGS can deliver on par or superior rendering quality within 100 seconds on a single RTX 5090 GPU card (up to 10x training speedup over vanilla 3DGS).

</details>


### [151] [GOOSE-M2F: Adapting Mask2Former for High-Fidelity, Long-Tailed Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation in Unstructured Outdoor Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15937)
*Jyothiraditya Lingam,Nikhileswara Rao Sulake,Sai Manikanta Eswar Machara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOOSE-M2F是基于Mask2Former改进的2D细粒度语义分割模型，通过增加查询数量、特征细化模块、辅助监督头和多阶段训练策略，在GOOSE挑战赛中达到70.08%的复合mIoU，排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GOOSE基准数据集包含64个细粒度类别，存在严重的长尾分布问题，稀有类别每张图像中像素数少于50个，标准Mask2Former难以应对这种极端不平衡场景。

Method: (1) 将对象查询扩展到200个以消除表示饱和；(2) 引入结合ASPP-lite和CBAM双注意力的特征细化模块；(3) 添加辅助监督头为稀有类别提供直接逐像素梯度；(4) 多阶段训练策略结合分布平衡损失、稀有类复制粘贴增强、动态IoU加权和EMA；(5) 推理时采用密集滑动窗口引擎、2D高斯核混合和4尺度TTA。

Result: GOOSE-M2F在GOOSE 2D FGSS排行榜上达到70.08%官方复合mIoU（细粒度63.55%，粗粒度76.61%），排名第三。

Conclusion: 通过针对长尾分布的三种关键改进（更多查询、特征细化、辅助监督）及多阶段训练策略，有效提升了细粒度语义分割在极端不平衡场景下的性能。

Abstract: We present GOOSE-M2F, a task-specific adaptation of Mask2Former for the GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation (FGSS) Challenge at ICRA~2026. The GOOSE benchmark spans 64 fine-grained classes across unstructured outdoor terrain with a severely long-tailed distribution, where rare classes occupy fewer than 50 pixels per image. We extend the Swin-Large Mask2Former baseline with three targeted contributions: (1)200 Object Queries to eliminate representational saturation; (2)a Feature Refinement Module (FRM) combining ASPP-lite and CBAM dual-attention; and (3)an Auxiliary Supervision Head that delivers direct per-pixel gradients for rare classes. A multi-stage training strategy pairs Distribution-Balanced loss, Rare-Class Copy-Paste augmentation, dynamic IoU-aware re-weighting, and EMA. At inference, a dense sliding-window engine with 2D Gaussian kernel blending and 4-scale TTA adds +10.57\%. GOOSE-M2F achieves 70.08\% Official Composite mIoU (63.55\% fine, 76.61\% coarse), placing 3rd on the GOOSE 2D FGSS leaderboard. Code and trained models are publicly available at: \href{https://github.com/Aditya-Lingam-9000/GOOSE-M2F}{Github GOOSE-M2F Code} and \href{https://huggingface.co/XYZ9843/GOOSE-M2F}{Hugging Face GOOSE-M2F}.

</details>


### [152] [You Don't Need Strong Assumptions: Visual Representation Learning via Temporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15956)
*Ninad Daithankar,Alexi Gladstone,Yann LeCun,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉表征学习中归纳偏置随数据规模增大而成为瓶颈的现象，并提出了一种基于因果假设的新型自监督学习范式TDV（Temporal Difference in Vision），通过联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，在不依赖强归纳偏置的情况下实现了与最先进方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI领域的趋势是弱归纳偏置方法随数据和算力增长表现更好，但现有自监督学习方法仍依赖数据增强、掩码、裁剪等强归纳偏置。作者推测这些偏置在更大规模数据下会成为瓶颈，因此探索更少假设的表征学习方法。

Method: 提出TDV（Temporal Difference in Vision）范式：联合训练图像编码器和运动编码器，使得当前帧的表征加上编码的运动信息等于下一帧的表征，核心假设是"过去导致未来"这一因果关系，而不使用传统强归纳偏置。

Result: 实验证实归纳偏置的最优强度随数据增长而降低。TDV在不使用任何强归纳偏置的情况下，在密集空间任务上达到了与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 验证了弱归纳偏置方法在大规模数据下的优势，TDV为不依赖强假设的表征学习奠定了新基础，展示了从视频中进行因果学习的潜力。

Abstract: Progress in AI has largely been driven by methods that assume less. As compute and data increase, approaches with weaker inductive biases generally outperform those with stronger assumptions. This is particularly characteristic of the field of Visual Representation Learning, where approaches have gone from being dominated by Supervised Learning, to Weakly Supervised Learning, to the now widespread success of Self-Supervised Learning without human labels. Yet, even modern Self-Supervised Learning approaches still depend on strong inductive biases such as augmentations, masking, or cropping. If this trend holds, even these remaining biases should become bottlenecks at scale -- and our experiments confirm this: the optimal strength of inductive biases decreases as data grows. This motivates the search for approaches that rely on fewer assumptions. To this end, we introduce Temporal Difference in Vision (TDV), a new paradigm for self-supervised learning from video that avoids existing inductive biases, relying instead on a causal assumption that the past causes the future. TDV functions by jointly training an image encoder and a motion encoder so that the current frame's representation plus the encoded motion equals the next frame's representation. Despite not leveraging any strong inductive biases, TDV matches state-of-the-art recipes on dense spatial tasks, laying the foundation for representation learning without strong assumptions.

</details>


### [153] [VEPHand: View-Efficient Photometric Hand Performance Capture at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15966)
*Zhengyang Shen,Kai-Hung Chang,Erroll Wood,Deying Kong,Bo Peng,Timo Bolkart,Jinlong Yang,Bowen Zhao,Danhang Tang,Sasa Petrovic,Emre Aksan,Jérémy Riviere,Vassilis Choutas,Delio Vicini,Jay Busch,Shichen Liu,Zhe Cao,Hugh Liu,JingJing Shen,Jonathan Taylor,Mingsong Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有限视角（约20个视图）的端到端3D手部动态捕捉与配准管线，通过无遮罩神经重建和物理启发式配准两大创新，解决了几何模糊、自接触变形等难题，在12000+序列上验证了高保真重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图手部捕捉系统需要在丰富光度信息与有限视角密度导致的几何模糊之间取得平衡，且传统方法依赖遮罩、难以处理严重自接触和非线性皮肤变形，因此需要一种在视图效率高的设置下仍能实现鲁棒、高保真动态手部捕捉的端到端方案。

Method: 1) 无遮罩神经重建方法：通过场景参数化和场景特定密度正则化，从无遮罩图像中鲁棒提取手部几何与外观；2) 物理启发式配准框架：在个性化手部模型的规范四面体网格内优化内在体积偏移和姿态参数，结合鲁棒损失函数捕捉精细表面变形，确保严重关节运动和自接触下的合理结果。

Result: 在超过12,000个序列的大规模数据集上验证了方法的可扩展性和鲁棒性，涵盖单手、双手交互和手物操作场景，在视图高效、无遮罩场景下实现了最先进的重建保真度和高精度配准。

Conclusion: 该自动化管线在有限视角设置下有效解决了手部重建的几何模糊和配准难题，生成的高质量合成数据集可辅助下游任务训练，展示了在复杂手部交互场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: Robust, high-fidelity 3D hand capture, while fundamental to digital human creation, remains challenging with practical multi-view systems that balance rich photometry with the geometric ambiguities of reconstruction arising from limited viewpoint density. This paper presents an end-to-end pipeline for dynamic hand performance capture and registration, specifically designed for view-efficient setups ($\sim$20 views). We address key challenges with two primary innovations. First, to overcome reconstruction difficulties like limited view overlap and background clutter, our mask-free neural method robustly extracts detailed hand geometry and appearance from unmasked images using scene parameterization and scenario-specific density regularization. Second, addressing registration challenges such as accurately capturing non-linear skin deformations and ensuring plausible results during severe self-contact, we propose a physics-inspired framework. It aligns reconstructions to a personalized hand model by optimizing intrinsic volumetric offsets within its canonical tetrahedral mesh, alongside pose parameters. This approach, supported by robust losses and optimization, captures fine surface deformations, ensures plausible results under severe articulation and self-contact, and demonstrates strong tolerance to input noise. We demonstrate the scalability and robustness of our automated pipeline on an extensive dataset of over 12,000 sequences, from which we also derive a large-scale, high-quality synthetic 2D/3D hand dataset for training downstream tasks. This showcases its effectiveness for single hands, intricate two-hand interactions, and natural hand-object manipulations. Our method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity in view-efficient, unmasked scenarios and highly accurate registration. Our project page are available at https://zyshen021.github.io/VEPHand/.

</details>


### [154] [CRIS: Cross-Plane Self-Supervised Isotropic Restoration for Anisotropic Volumetric Imaging Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15967)
*Adi Ahituv,Anat Ilivitzki,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRIS是一种跨平面自监督框架，无需配对各向同性真实数据即可实现3D各向同性恢复。通过在正交重采样上执行2D条纹补全，在MRI和体积电子显微镜数据上均优于现有方法，支持高达8倍各向异性的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床MRI和体积电子显微镜中常见的各向异性体素采集由于平面间采样稀疏，导致厚切片或厚截面，降低了正交重采样质量和下游分析性能。现有方法通常需要配对的各向同性真实数据或特定配置重新训练。

Method: CRIS将3D恢复转化为对各向同性网格正交重采样的2D条纹补全：训练时对高分辨率面内切片进行合成退化并周期性掩码；推理时空白切片定义各向同性网格，恢复两个正交重采样并通过多视角平均融合预测。

Result: 在脑MRI上PSNR 32.921 dB、SSIM 0.9631，分割Dice 0.940；无参考腹部MRI的FID/KID降至48.714/0.023；vEM上4倍各向异性PSNR 29.133 dB、SSIM 0.834；鲁棒性实验中在3-7倍间隙因子和不同退化方向上均优于插值方法。

Conclusion: CRIS提供了一种模态灵活的途径实现各向同性恢复，无需配对目标或特定配置重新训练，代码已公开。

Abstract: Anisotropic volumetric acquisitions are common in clinical MRI and volume electron microscopy (vEM), where sparse through-plane sampling creates thick slices or sections that degrade orthogonal reformats and downstream analysis. We present CRIS, a cross-plane self-supervised framework for isotropic restoration without paired isotropic ground truth. CRIS casts 3D restoration as 2D stripe completion on orthogonal reformats of an isotropic grid: high-resolution in-plane slices are synthetically degraded and periodically masked for training, while at inference blank slices define the isotropic grid, two orthogonal reformats are restored, and predictions are fused by multi-view averaging. We evaluate CRIS on two MRI cohorts and two microscopy benchmarks up to 8x anisotropy. On brain MRI, CRIS achieves 32.921 +/- 0.436 dB PSNR and 0.9631 +/- 0.0027 SSIM, outperforming interpolation, SMORE4, SIMPLE, SA-INR, and ATME, and gives the best segmentation consistency (Dice 0.940 +/- 0.004, ASSD 0.245 +/- 0.014 mm, HD99 1.275 +/- 0.061 mm). On reference-free abdominal MRI, CRIS reduces FID/KID to 48.714/0.023. On vEM, CRIS outperforms interpolation, NIIV, and vEMINR, reaching 29.133 dB/0.834 3D PSNR/SSIM at 4x, 27.123 dB/0.734 on EPFL at 8x, and 21.915 dB/0.699 on noisy hemibrain data. In a robustness experiment, one variable-gap CRIS model evaluated across gap factors 3--7 and coronal, axial, and sagittal degradations maintained higher PSNR/SSIM than interpolation (36.36--31.14 dB and 0.977--0.932 vs. 33.07--27.85 dB and 0.951--0.853). These results support CRIS as a modality-flexible route to isotropic restoration without paired isotropic targets or configuration-specific retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/adi-hatav/CRIS.

</details>


### [155] [HadBalance: A Plug-and-Play Unified Global Geometric Prior Framework for Generalizable Biomedical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15976)
*Zhuangzhi Gao,Feixiang Zhou,He Zhao,Wenhan Chen,Ruiyu Luo,Xin Wang,Hongyi Qin,Zhongli Wu,Yanda Meng,Yitian Zhao,Alena Shantsila,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Eduard Shantsila,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HadBalance框架，利用Hadwiger形状先验（面积、周长、欧拉特征数）作为全局正则化器，并设计冲突感知目标平衡（CAOB）方法，在不破坏真实凹性细节的前提下，实现跨器官和模态的医学图像分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何先验方法大多任务特化，缺乏统一的几何基础来泛化到不同器官和模态；同时，直接施加近凸先验会过度正则化非凸解剖结构，导致分割精度下降。

Method: 基于Hadwiger定理推导出三种2D度量（面积A、周长P、欧拉示性数χ）作为可解释的全局形状正则化器；提出冲突感知目标平衡（CAOB），以梯度感知方式整合形状先验与分割目标，仅移除与分割冲突的梯度分量，保留对齐部分，自适应调节目标权重防止先验主导。

Result: 该方法能在形状异质的数据上稳定使用形状先验，不会抹除真实凹性或精细结构细节，实现跨器官和模态的分割性能提升。

Conclusion: HadBalance是一个即插即用的通用框架，通过统一的几何先验和梯度级冲突平衡，有效解决了医学图像分割中形状异质性的难题。

Abstract: Precise biomedical image segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis. Geometric cues (e.g., boundary, shape, and topology) can improve structural consistency, yet most are task-specific and lack a unified geometric foundation that generalizes across organs and modalities. We are motivated by the observation that several medical segmentation targets can be approximated as globally near-convex shapes. A convex region is one in which any two interior points can be connected by a line segment entirely contained within the region. In practice, medical targets may exhibit small local concavities or boundary irregularities; we refer to such globally convex-like shapes as near-convex. Motivated by this, we derive Hadwiger Shape Priors from Hadwiger's theorem as an interpretable global regularizer using three 2D measures: area A, perimeter P, and Euler characteristic chi, enabling transfer across organs and modalities. However, because medical datasets are shape-heterogeneous, enforcing near-convex priors uniformly can over-regularize non-convex anatomy with significant concavities, washing out concavities and fine details and degrading segmentation accuracy. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Aware Objective Balancing (CAOB), which integrates shape priors with segmentation in a gradient-aware manner. For each prior, CAOB removes only the gradient component that conflicts with segmentation while preserving the remaining aligned component, and adaptively regulates objective influences to prevent prior dominance. This enables stable use of shape priors on shape-heterogeneous data without erasing genuine concavities or fine structural details. We call this plug-and-play framework HadBalance.

</details>


### [156] [Mind the Gap: Diagnosing Constraint Discovery Failures in Text-in-Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15982)
*Rui Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态推理中模型自主发现未明确视觉依赖关系的能力，发现模型在无引导下仅恢复46%的约束，而显式提供约束时达94%，表明失败主要源于无法自主发现隐含依赖。因果解释是最有效的部分引导，且更高的自主发现召回率并不一定提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理的关键挑战在于确定特定任务下哪些视觉依赖关系是相关的，而不仅仅是识别可见内容。作者通过文本图像编辑中的编辑引发约束发现这一受控诊断设定来研究该问题。

Method: 构建461个诊断案例、4个多模态大模型和19种约束子类型，通过对比无引导提示、显式提供约束以及Oracle字段分解（因果解释、区域名称、类型标签）来评估模型性能。

Result: 无引导下模型恢复46%的案例级宏召回率，显式提供约束时达94%。因果解释是最有效的部分引导（0.782召回率），高于区域名称（0.610）和类型标签（0.646）。更高的自主发现召回率并不一定提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 模型在多模态推理中失败的主要原因是无法自主发现隐含的依赖关系；因果线索能有效弥补这一缺陷；需要精度感知的约束提取方法，因为未验证的自主发现会引入假阳性抵消召回率增益。

Abstract: A key challenge in multimodal reasoning is determining which visual dependencies become relevant under a specific task, rather than merely recognizing visible content. We study this through edit-induced constraint discovery in text-in-image editing, a controlled diagnostic setting where a local text change can activate secondary consistency constraints: given a valid editing instruction and an image, can a model identify the secondary regions that must also change? Across 461 diagnostic cases, four MLLMs, and 19 constraint subtypes, models recover only 46% case-level macro recall under unguided prompting versus 94% when constraints are explicitly provided, suggesting that a substantial portion of the failure arises when models must decide which unstated dependencies to surface. Oracle-field decomposition shows that case-specific causal explanations are the most effective partial guidance (0.782 recall), above region names (0.610) or type labels (0.646), suggesting that edit-specific causal cues account for much of the oracle gain. A downstream experiment further shows that higher self-discovery recall does not necessarily improve task performance: unverified self-discovery introduces false positives that offset recall gains, motivating precision-aware constraint elicitation.

</details>


### [157] [A Text Recognition Dataset from Sahidic Coptic Ancient Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15987)
*Fabio Quattrini,Carmine Zaccagnino,Costanza Bianchi,Silvia Cascianelli,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SCAM（Sahidic Coptic Ancient Manuscripts）数据集，这是一个针对低资源手写文本识别（HTR）场景的新数据集，包含已灭绝的Sahidic Coptic方言的数字化古代手稿，并基准测试了多种主流HTR方法，揭示了当前方法在该场景下的局限性与未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HTR研究多聚焦于资源丰富的现代语言，而面向低资源场景（如濒危语言、罕见文字、历史文献退化）的研究不足。作者旨在填补这一空白，为低资源HTR提供一个真实且具有挑战性的基准。

Method: 构建了SCAM行级数据集，包含来自多个图书馆数字化馆藏的Sahidic Coptic手稿，涵盖墨水褪色、透印、材料老化等退化特征。基于不同范式（如CTC、Attention等）的多种主流HTR方法在该数据集上进行基准测试与对比分析。

Result: 实验表明，当前主流HTR方法在低资源历史手稿场景下的性能远低于在资源丰富的现代脚本上的表现，揭示了显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: 低资源HTR仍是一个重大挑战，SCAM数据集为未来方法改进提供了有价值的参考基准，呼吁研究者关注真实历史文献场景下的识别技术发展。

Abstract: In this work, we target Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) in low-resource scenarios, which arise from underrepresented languages, rare scripts, and degraded visual conditions typical of historical documents. We introduce SCAM (Sahidic Coptic Ancient Manuscripts), a new line-level dataset built from digitized ancient manuscripts written in the extinct Sahidic Coptic dialect. The dataset reflects a realistic and challenging setting, as it combines heterogeneous acquisition conditions across libraries with typical manuscript degradations such as ink fading, bleed-through, and material deterioration. In addition to visual complexity, SCAM poses significant linguistic challenges due to the scarcity of resources for Sahidic Coptic, its uncommon alphabet, and dialect-specific diacritics. To support research in low-resource HTR, we benchmark several state-of-the-art approaches based on different paradigms, highlighting their limitations and strengths in this setting. Our results underline the gap between current HTR performance on well-resourced modern scripts and historically grounded, low-resource scenarios, thus providing a reference point for future developments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801)
*Yifan Ruan,Chenyang Cao,Andreas Burger,Ali Pesaranghader,Kaveh Kamali,Jaehong Kim,Nandita Vijaykumar,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Igor Gilitschenski,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QPILOTS方法，在不修改原始扩散/流匹配策略的情况下，通过在推理时利用评论家梯度引导去噪过程，实现了高效的时序差分强化学习策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将流匹配/扩散策略与TD强化学习结合时存在数值不稳定性，要么丢弃梯度信息，要么将策略蒸馏为单步actor，或随着评论家改进反复微调去噪策略，缺乏一种稳定且不修改原始策略的方法。

Method: 提出QPILOTS，在推理时每个去噪步骤中，先将带噪中间状态投影到最终干净动作的估计，再在该估计上计算评论家梯度来引导去噪过程。包含两种变体：QPILOTS-U（快速单点近似）和QPILOTS-M（通过学习辅助网络进行可微分后验采样）。

Result: 在标准离线到在线强化学习基准上，在50个任务中平均成功率达到90%，取得最佳综合表现。在冻结的预训练VLA基础模型上，在6个仿真操作任务中超越或匹配了先前推理时方法。

Conclusion: QPILOTS是一种无需修改原始策略、在推理时有效利用评论家梯度引导扩散/流匹配策略的方法，在离线到在线RL和VLA模型微调中均表现优异。

Abstract: Flow-matching and diffusion policies are expressive action generators, but optimizing them with temporal-difference reinforcement learning (RL) remains difficult. Effective policy extraction requires exploiting the critic's action gradient, yet directly backpropagating this signal through a multi-step denoising process can be numerically unstable. Existing methods work around this either by discarding gradient information, distilling the policy into a simpler one-step actor, or repeatedly fine-tuning the denoising policy as the critic improves. We propose QPILOTS, a method that leaves the original policy unmodified and steers the denoising process at inference time. At each denoising step, instead of evaluating the critic on the noisy intermediate action where critic predictions are unreliable, we first project that intermediate state to an estimate of the final clean action and compute the critic gradient there. We introduce two variants: QPILOTS-U uses a fast single-point approximation, while QPILOTS-M draws differentiable posterior samples via a learned auxiliary network. On a standard offline-to-online RL benchmark, QPILOTS achieves the best aggregate performance, reaching an average success rate of 90% across 50 tasks. We also apply QPILOTS to steer a large, frozen, pretrained Vision-Language Action (VLA) foundation model, outperforming or matching prior inference-time approaches across six manipulation tasks in simulation.

</details>


### [159] [GRAPE: Guided Parameter-Space Evolution for Compact Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14865)
*Zhiyuan Ye,Xiangyu Zhou,Ji Qi,Hao Zhang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAPE框架，通过渐进式参数空间暴露与引导式演化，在相近计算量下显著提升对抗鲁棒性（PGD-20从51.70%提升至56.94%），同时减少21.4%参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数变得可优化的顺序是否会影响最终的鲁棒解，即使最终架构或计算预算被控制。

Method: GRAPE（Guided Parameter-Space Evolution）：结合参数空间稳定化与渐进式隐藏扩张，利用对抗频谱利用率分数引导新释放的容量流向高压模块。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，固定结构ResNet-18 AT为基准（51.70%），GRAPE达到56.94% PGD-20鲁棒准确率，FLOPs比1.009x，参数量减少21.4%；顺序增长变体在相同ResNet-18架构下达到56.52%。

Conclusion: 引导式参数空间演化能在匹配计算量下产生紧凑且鲁棒的参数配置，参数空间暴露路径本身对鲁棒性提升有贡献。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) improves neural network robustness, but most methods train a fixed parameter space from the start. This paper asks whether the order in which parameters become optimizable can affect the final robust solution, even when the final architecture or computation budget is controlled.
  We propose GRAPE, Guided Parameter-Space Evolution, a training framework for compact adversarial robustness. GRAPE combines parameter-space stabilization with progressive hidden expansion: it stabilizes robust optimization in the currently exposed space, gradually releases new optimizable dimensions, and uses an adversarial spectral utilization score to guide newly released capacity toward high-pressure modules. In contrast to fixed-structure AT, GRAPE treats robust model learning as a process of progressive parameter-space exposure and evolution.
  Under the standard $\ell_\infty$ threat model on CIFAR-10, with fixed-structure ResNet-18 AT as a controlled reference, GRAPE improves PGD-20 robust accuracy from 51.70% to 56.94% at a nearly matched computation budget with a FLOPs ratio of 1.009x, while reducing parameter count by about 21.4%. A sequential grow variant with the same final ResNet-18 architecture reaches 56.52% PGD-20 robust accuracy, indicating that the gain is not only due to final architecture differences but also to the parameter-space exposure path. These results suggest that guided parameter-space evolution can yield compact and robust parameter configurations under matched computation.

</details>


### [160] [α-Fair Insurance Pricing: A Fairness Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14898)
*Tianhe Zhang,Xiguang Liu,Peng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出α-FISP框架，通过带预算约束的优化模型在精算公平与团结公平之间进行权衡，同时保证保险公司的偿付能力，并通过理论推导和数值实验验证了其可行性与监管兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险定价中长期存在精算公平（按个体风险差异化定价）与团结公平（跨群体交叉补贴）之间的矛盾，现代大数据技术加剧了这一张力，监管机构面临保护弱势群体的压力，因此需要一个能够显式权衡这两种公平性并保证偿付能力的统一框架。

Method: 将定价问题建模为带约束的优化问题：在精算公平保费的基础上，加入对各风险类别内交叉补贴的预算约束，通过参数α生成从纯精算公平到纯团结公平的连续解空间，并推导了理论保证。

Result: α-FISP框架在计算上是可处理的，数值实验表明其与具有不同州级公平要求的美国监管制度高度兼容。

Conclusion: α-FISP提供了一个可调节的保险定价框架，能够灵活地在精算公平与团结公平之间取得平衡，同时满足偿付能力要求，具备实际监管应用潜力。

Abstract: Fairness in insurance pricing remains a long-standing and deeply debated puzzle. On one hand, insurers, driven by profitability considerations, set premiums that differentiate across individual risks to achieve actuarial fairness. On the other hand, insurance serves a critical societal function by pooling risks across a population, motivating cross-subsidization among groups to promote solidarity fairness. The tension between these two competing notions of fairness makes insurance pricing inherently complex, particularly in modern settings where granular data allow for increasingly fine risk differentiation and regulators face growing pressure to protect vulnerable groups. To address this challenge, we propose an $α$-\textbf{F}air \textbf{I}ndividual \textbf{S}olvent \textbf{P}remium ($α$-FISP) framework for insurance pricing that explicitly captures the trade-off between actuarial and solidarity fairness while guaranteeing solvency, a fundamental requirement in insurance operations. We formulate the pricing problem as a constrained optimization task, where actuarially fair premiums are adjusted subject to budget constraints on cross-subsidization within each risk class. This formulation naturally yields a family of solutions parameterized by $α$, tracing a continuum between purely actuarial and purely solidarity-based pricing and enabling decision-makers to select an operating point along this fairness spectrum. We derive theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework. Numerical experiments show that $α$-FISP is computationally tractable and aligns well with the U.S. regulatory regimes featuring heterogeneous state-level fairness requirements.

</details>


### [161] [GRASP: Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer for Memory-Efficient Multi-Source Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14900)
*Mary Isabelle Wisell,Nicholas Jacobs,Aayush Manandhar,Salimeh Yasaei Sekeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRASP方法，通过顺序处理、参数级梯度对齐和迭代微调，实现O(1)内存消耗的多源迁移学习，在三个基准测试中达到93.5%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多源迁移学习存在O(K)内存瓶颈：参数融合时需要同时加载K个源模型，或推理时部署所有模型，导致资源受限场景下不可行。

Method: (1) 顺序处理：每次仅合并一个源模型到目标模型；(2) 参数级梯度对齐：仅转移与目标域优化方向一致的有用参数；(3) 迭代微调：集成下一源模型前先适应已迁移知识。

Result: 在Yearbook、CLEAR-10、CLEAR-100基准上，GRASP达到93.5%平均准确率（集成方法71.7%），内存保持O(1)常数。

Conclusion: GRASP可扩展至任意数量源模型且内存不增长，特别适合资源受限场景和持续演化的源域部署。

Abstract: Multi-source transfer learning faces a fundamental scalability bottleneck: existing approaches require either loading all K source models into memory simultaneously during parameter fusion, requiring O(K) memory, or deploying all models at inference time, making production deployment infeasible. We propose GRASP (Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer), which achieves superior knowledge integration while maintaining O(1) memory consumption through three key innovations: (1) sequential processing that merges one source at a time into an evolving target model, (2) parameter-wise gradient alignment that selectively transfers only parameters whose optimization directions align with the target domain, avoiding negative transfer, and (3) iterative fine-tuning that adapts transferred knowledge before integrating the next source. Extensive experiments across three continual learning benchmarks (Yearbook, CLEAR-10, CLEAR-100) spanning 10 to 108-year temporal distribution shifts and four architectures (1.3M to 25.6M parameters) demonstrate that GRASP achieves 93.5% mean accuracy over all datasets and architectures compared to ensemble method's 71.7% accuracy while requiring only constant memory versus K models for standard multi-source fusion. Critically, GRASP's sequential previously merged models and scales to arbitrarily many sources without memory growth, making it uniquely suitable for resource-constrained deployment and continually evolving source domains.

</details>


### [162] [Policy Regret for Embedding Model Routing: Contextual Bandits with Low-Rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14929)
*Yan Dai,Negin Golrezaei,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了嵌入模型路由作为带低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性老虎机问题，提出了一种名为Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG)的策略梯度算法，该算法在理论上可证明适应未知低秩结构，并实现了$\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$的线性化策略遗憾，避免了维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统依赖将多样查询动态路由到多个嵌入模型，但该问题在对抗性查询、强盗反馈和模型可观测性有限等现实条件下缺乏理论理解。

Method: 将嵌入模型路由形式化为带低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性老虎机；识别出对数-二次策略类以平衡表达能力与可学习性；提出Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG)算法，可自适应未知低秩结构。

Result: HPG算法在线性化策略遗憾上达到$\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$，其中$s$为专家内在秩，$M$为模型数，$T$为轮数，避免了维度灾难。

Conclusion: 该工作为嵌入模型路由问题提供了理论框架和高效算法，证明了在对抗性设置下实现可证明的在线学习的可能性。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems increasingly rely on dynamically routing diverse queries to multiple embedding models. Despite its practical significance, this problem remains poorly understood under realistic conditions like adversarial queries, bandit feedback, and limited observability of models. We formalize embedding model routing as an adversarial contextual linear bandit with low-rank experts, where contexts are queries, actions are items, and experts are the embedding models working on low-rank latent representation spaces. We first establish that standard regret notions suffer from structural misspecification or statistical intractability, and we identify a log-quadratic policy class that is expressive enough to capture query-dependent model routing, yet structured enough to allow efficient online learning. Second, we propose a policy gradient algorithm called Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG). It provably adapts to the unknown low-rank structure under incomplete information and attains $\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$ linearized policy regret -- where $s, M$, and $T$ are the intrinsic rank of the experts, the number of models, and the number of rounds -- thus avoiding a curse of dimensionality. Finally, we also provide an computationally efficient and parameter-free implementation of HPG.

</details>


### [163] [Separable Neural Architectures as Physical World Models: from Mathematical Theory to Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14934)
*Reza T Batley,Andrew Kichline,Sourav Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分离神经架构（SNA），将神经网络与张量分解结合，通过解耦局部坐标函数和全局稀疏低秩交互，实现高效求解高维时空参数偏微分方程，在7维制造仿真中实现150,000倍加速，支持实时逆问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络求解PDE时面临维度灾难，高维问题计算成本指数增长；现有方法缺乏严格的变分理论保证，且难以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出分离神经架构（SNA），将坐标函数（原子）与全局稀疏低秩交互对象解耦；构建变分SNA（VSNA）框架满足Lax-Milgram变分保证；采用完全因子化的交替最小二乘（ALS）优化，计算复杂度与维度呈线性关系。

Result: 在7维参数制造仿真中，VSNA在普通笔记本电脑CPU上102秒完成100万次蒙特卡洛采样，相比A100 GPU上的全网格有限元基线实现150,000倍加速；支持100ms内实时生成式逆模式重建。

Conclusion: SNA为连续参数流形提供紧凑的数学基底，可实现实时反演、优化循环和快速不确定性传播，是"一次求解，随处查询"的物理世界模型。

Abstract: This work introduces the Separable Neural Architecture (SNA), a function representational class combining neural approximation with tensor decomposition. The SNA decouples localized coordinate functions (atoms) from global interactions governed by a sparse, low-rank interaction object. This architecture possesses a compact and smooth inductive bias well-suited for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). When viewed as a Galerkin trial space under the variational SNA (VSNA) framework, the formulation satisfies classical variational guarantees under Lax-Milgram: well-posedness, quasi-optimality, convergence, and stability. In high-dimensional spatiotemporal--parametric PDEs, the VSNA mitigates the curse of dimensionality by scaling algebraically rather than exponentially. Exploiting an entirely factorized, tensor-native alternating least squares (ALS) optimization framework reduces this cost to linear in dimension. The VSNA is validated across elliptic, hyperbolic, and parabolic systems, demonstrating close alignment with predicted algebraic and spectral scaling rates. We showcase the SNA as a "solve once, query anywhere" physical world model via two engineering case studies: a 7D parametric manufacturing simulation and an experimental thermal-to-property inversion pipeline for Inconel 718. The VSNA executes a 1,000,000-query Monte Carlo sweep in 102s on a standard laptop CPU, yielding a 150,000x speedup over a full-grid finite element baseline hosted on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. It further enables real-time generative inverse-mode reconstructions under 100ms. These results demonstrate that the SNA serves as a compact mathematical substrate for continuous parameter manifolds to enable real-time inversion, optimization loops, and rapid uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [164] [Remember, Don't Re-read: Stateful ReAct Agents for Token-Efficient Autonomous Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14945)
*Faramarz Jabbarvaziri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将无状态的自动研究模式重构为基于LangGraph的状态化ReAct智能体，通过持久化状态和工具调用接口，在超参数调优和代码性能优化任务上分别减少90%和52%的令牌消耗，同时保持优化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动研究模式的无状态设计每个迭代都从零重建实验上下文，导致单次迭代O(n)令牌成本和总开销O(n²)，效率低下。

Method: 将自动研究模式重构为基于LangGraph的状态化ReAct智能体，使用类型化持久化状态跨迭代保存实验历史，通过工具调用接口在固定大小对话窗口内以O(1)成本运行。

Result: 超参数调优任务令牌消耗降低90%（2,492 vs 24,465），代码优化任务令牌消耗降低52%（627K vs 1,275K），且优化质量相当。

Conclusion: 状态化设计在保持任务性能的前提下显著降低令牌成本，其结构性优势在于将O(n)迭代成本降为O(1)，为实践者提供了可复现的实现架构。

Abstract: The autoresearch pattern enables autonomous experimentation by having a large language model (LLM) iteratively modify code to optimize a target metric. Its stateless design, however, reconstructs experimental context from scratch at every iteration, incurring $O(n)$ token cost per iteration and $O(n^{2})$ total. This work reformulates the pattern as a stateful ReAct agent using LangGraph, where typed persistent state carries experimental history across iterations via a tool-calling interface. Two benchmarks are evaluated: hyperparameter tuning (15 iterations, small per-iteration observations) and code performance optimization (40 iterations, large per-iteration observations containing full source code and benchmark results). On hyperparameter tuning, the stateful agent consumes 90\% fewer tokens (2{,}492 vs.\ 24{,}465). On code optimization, the stateful agent consumes 52\% fewer tokens (627K vs.\ 1{,}275K) while achieving comparable optimization quality on both tasks. The token reduction is structural: the stateless agent re-reads the full history at $O(n)$ cost per iteration, while the stateful agent operates within a fixed-size conversation window at $O(1)$ cost. This paper describes the architecture in sufficient detail for practitioners to implement a stateful autoresearch agent for their own workflows.

</details>


### [165] [A Comparative Study of Graph Neural Network Layer Selection for Interaction Modelling in Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14956)
*George Daoud,Mohamed El-Darieby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了19种图神经网络层在轨迹预测任务中的效果，发现ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现最佳，并总结了sum聚合、多头注意力和不同跳距权重等设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹预测是自动驾驶的关键模块，虽然GNN已广泛用于建模智能体间的时空交互，但其架构设计缺乏标准化，对于哪些图层的空间和时间处理能力最优缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 对19种图神经网络层进行详细的比较研究，聚焦其空间交互捕获和时间动态建模能力，在固定超参数设置下评估各层组合的性能差异。

Result: ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层在性能上持续优于其他层，发现了五种突出的层组合；sum聚合优于mean聚合，多头注意力机制能实现更丰富的交互，对不同跳距赋予不同权重可显著提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计更可解释、更有效的轨迹预测模型提供了实用设计原则和架构选择指导。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely on precise trajectory prediction to plan safe and efficient movement. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become a promising approach for modelling spatiotemporal interactions among road agents. However, designing GNN architectures for trajectory prediction remains non-standardized, with little guidance on which graph layers effectively capture spatial interactions and temporal dynamics. This paper offers a detailed comparative study of 19 graph layer types, focusing on their spatial and temporal processing capabilities to discover the most effective architectures for trajectory prediction. Within the explored hyperparameter setting, we highlight five standout layer combinations, with ARMA, Chebyshev, and topology-aware layers consistently performing better than others. Beyond performance metrics, our findings yield practical design principles: sum-based aggregation is more effective than mean-based methods, multi-head attention mechanisms enable richer interactions, and assigning different weights to different hop distances significantly improves prediction accuracy. These findings offer useful guidance for designing more interpretable and effective trajectory prediction models.

</details>


### [166] [Leveraging Physiological Signals to Predict Exam Outcomes with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14960)
*Lala Yamazaki,Ramchandra Rimal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究利用机器学习模型分析考试中的生理信号（皮电活动、心率、皮肤温度）来预测学业表现，发现随机森林等简单模型在某些情况下优于深度学习模型，且Transformer表现与LSTM、GRU相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过生理指标与学业表现之间的关联分析，理解学生考试压力对心理健康的影响，并探索利用生理数据提升学生福祉与学业成果的可能性。

Method: 采集考试期间的皮电活动、心率、皮肤温度等生理指标，分别使用逻辑回归、随机森林、支持向量机等传统模型，以及Transformer、LSTM、GRU等深度学习模型进行预测，采用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数进行评估。

Result: 深度学习模型在捕捉生理数据复杂关系上表现优异，但随机森林等简单模型在某些情况下性能更优且更具计算效率和可解释性；Transformer展现出与LSTM、GRU相当的性能。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在解决实际问题时应广泛尝试不同类型模型，平衡精度、效率与可解释性；揭示了生理信号与学业表现之间的关联，为促进学生的心理健康和学业成果提供了支持。

Abstract: This study investigates the application of machine learning models to predict exam outcomes using physiological data collected during examination sessions. Physiological stress indicators, including electrodermal activity, heart rate, and skin temperature, were analyzed to uncover their association with academic performance. A variety of machine learning approaches were employed, ranging from standard models like logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machines to more advanced architectures, including transformers, long short-term memory (LSTM), and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models. This diversity aimed to capture the complex interactions within the data effectively. A key focus was assessing the adaptability of transformers in processing numerical data and evaluating their performance in this novel context. Standard performance metrics, such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, were used to compare model efficacy. The experimental results demonstrate that while deep learning models generally excel at capturing complex relationships in physiological data, simpler models like random forests can sometimes achieve superior performance while offering computational efficiency and interpretability. Furthermore, transformers demonstrated notable versatility, showcasing performances comparable to those of the LSTM and GRU models. This research underscores the importance of experimenting with a broad class of models that align with the objectives of the problem at hand, balancing precision, efficiency, and interpretability. By elucidating the relationships between physiological signals and academic performance, this study contributes to understanding stressors affecting students' mental health. It further promotes leveraging physiological data to enhance student well-being and academic outcomes.

</details>


### [167] [Benchmarking Instance-Dependent Label Noise with Controlled Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14965)
*Shadman Islam,Agustinus Kristiadi,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CILN框架通过可控的输入损坏生成实例依赖的标签噪声基准，比传统方法更透明地模拟真实标注歧义，并能暴露主流去噪学习方法（如Co-Teaching、DivideMix）在特定噪声结构下的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实例依赖标签噪声（IDN）基准生成方法通常依赖不完美标注者或分类器生成噪声，歧义来源不明确。本文旨在提出一个通过可控输入损坏生成IDN的框架，使噪声来源和严重程度可解释、可控制。

Method: 提出CILN框架：通过对干净样本施加可控的输入损坏（多种损坏族和严重级别），由多样化的投票者池对损坏样本进行标注，从而生成具有显式歧义来源的基准数据集。在CIFAR10、MNIST、Adult上构建了90种基准设置。

Result: CILN生成了真正的实例依赖噪声，具有多样化的混淆结构；在CIFAR-10上能产生比现有合成IDN基准更接近人类不确定性的标签分布；能够暴露Co-Teaching和DivideMix等方法在类似噪声率下未观察到的失败模式。

Conclusion: 噪声结构（而非仅噪声率）对基准难度和算法行为有重要影响。CILN通过使歧义生成显式且可控，为研究不同实例困难来源下的噪声标签学习提供了互补的基准框架。

Abstract: Synthetic instance-dependent label noise (IDN) benchmarks are widely used to evaluate noisy-label learning methods, yet existing approaches typically generate noise through imperfect annotators or classifier raters, leaving the source of ambiguity implicit. We introduce CILN, a benchmark generation framework that creates IDN through controlled input corruptions. A diverse voter pool labels corrupted instances, producing benchmark datasets in which both the source and severity of ambiguity are explicit and controllable. Using CIFAR10, MNIST, and Adult, we construct 90 benchmark settings spanning multiple corruption families and severity levels. Our experiments show that the resulting benchmarks exhibit genuine instance-dependent noise, provide diverse confusion structures, and, on CIFAR-10, can produce label distributions that are closer to human uncertainty than an existing synthetic IDN benchmark. We further demonstrate that corruption-mediated IDN can expose failure modes of popular noisy-label learning methods, including Co-Teaching and DivideMix, that are not observed under comparable levels of rater-fallibility noise. These findings suggest that noise structure, not only noise rate, plays an important role in benchmark difficulty and algorithm behavior. By making ambiguity generation explicit and controllable, CILN provides a complementary benchmarking framework for studying noisy-label learning under diverse sources of instance difficulty.

</details>


### [168] [Zero-order Parameter-free Optimization for LMO-based Methods: Novel Approach for Efficient Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14970)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaNAGED方法，统一了零阶优化、参数自适应和非欧几里得更新几何，用于大语言模型的高效微调，在OPT-1.3B模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调中反向传播的内存开销巨大，零阶优化虽然节省内存但对超参数敏感，参数自由优化可解决调参问题，而大规模微调还需要考虑参数块的几何异质性。

Method: 提出AdaNAGED方法，将参数自由自适应与基于线性最小化或acles的零阶优化相结合，实现无梯度训练、自适应调参和非欧几里得更新几何的统一。

Result: 建立了收敛性保证，并在OPT-1.3B模型的大规模LLM微调任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: AdaNAGED成功解决了零阶优化在LLM微调中超参数敏感和几何结构利用不足的问题，为内存高效的模型微调提供了新方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) has become a central application of modern optimization, enabling pretrained models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks and domain-specific data. A major obstacle in large-scale fine-tuning is the memory overhead of backpropagation, which requires storing activations, gradients, and optimizer states. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative, but its performance is highly sensitive to the stepsize and smoothing parameter, often requiring costly task-specific tuning. Parameter-free (PF) optimization addresses this issue by adapting algorithmic parameters without prior knowledge of problem-dependent constants. Moreover, large-scale fine-tuning can benefit from geometry-aware updates that account for the heterogeneous structure of parameter blocks, which can be modeled through methods that exploit linear minimization oracle (LMO). In this work, we study PF adaptation for LMO-based ZO optimization and introduce $\texttt{AdaNAGED}$, a method that unifies gradient-free training, adaptive tuning, and non-Euclidean update geometry. We establish convergence guarantees and validate the method on large-scale LLM fine-tuning task with $\texttt{OPT}-1.3\mathrm{B}$ model.

</details>


### [169] [FastMix: Fast Data Mixture Optimization via Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14971)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Jun Xia,Ruobing Xie,Bin Xia,Xingwu Sun,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTMIX 是一个自动化数据混合发现框架，通过将混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，仅训练单个代理模型即可同时优化混合系数和模型参数，在预训练和后训练中均优于基线方法且大幅降低搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型依赖大规模多样化数据集，但如何确定预训练和后训练的最优数据混合比例仍是一个重大未解决问题。现有方法依赖预定义启发式或资源密集的模拟，效率低下。

Method: 将混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，证明优化混合比例等价于在均匀源采样下分配每源损失权重，从而实现梯度优化的差分迭代目标。采用近似迭代优化过程，交替更新模型参数（内循环）和基于验证反馈更新混合比例（外循环）。

Result: 在预训练和后训练场景中，FASTMIX 均优于基线方法，同时大幅降低了搜索成本。

Conclusion: FASTMIX 提供了一种高效、可扩展的自动化数据混合发现方法，避免了资源密集型模拟和预定义启发式，为大型模型的数据配比优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: While large and diverse datasets have driven recent advances in large models, identifying the optimal data mixture for pre-training and post-training remains a significant open problem. We address this challenge with FASTMIX, a novel framework that automates data mixture discovery while training only a single proxy model. Instead of relying on predefined heuristics or resource-intensive simulations, FASTMIX jointly optimizes mixture coefficients and model parameters, substantially improving efficiency and scalability over prior approaches. At the core of FASTMIX is a reformulation of mixture selection as a bilevel optimization problem. Under this reformulation, we show that optimizing mixture ratios is mathematically equivalent to assigning per-source loss weights under uniform source sampling. This embeds the mixture coefficients directly into the differentiable iterative optimization objective, enabling efficient, gradient-based optimization of both mixture and model. To solve the optimization problem, FASTMIX implements an approximate iterative optimization procedure, alternating between (i) updating model parameters on data sampled according to current mixture ratios (inner loop) and (ii) updating mixture ratios based on validation feedback (outer loop). Across pre- and post-training, FASTMIX outperforms baselines while drastically reducing search cost. Code (https://github.com/hrtan/fastmix)

</details>


### [170] [Rational Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14990)
*Naiyu Yin,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE)，用可训练的有理函数替代传统SAE中固定的编码器激活函数（如ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），通过初始化+微调两阶段流程，在多个语言模型和基线家族上一致提升了重构质量和下游行为指标，且仅增加少量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）使用固定的编码器非线性激活函数（如ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），硬编码了特定的稀疏机制，限制了模型适应预激活几何分布的能力，且在重构质量与稀疏性之间存在权衡扭曲。

Method: 提出Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE)，用可训练的有理函数替代固定激活函数。采用两阶段流程：1）初始化阶段：复制预训练基线SAE权重，通过松弛Remez交换算法在合成数据上拟合有理系数并校准尺度参数；2）微调阶段：在标准稀疏正则化重构目标下进行微调。

Result: 在三个开源语言模型的残差流激活上，针对所有三种基线激活家族，RSAE在微调后严格优于基线，在重构指标和下游行为指标上均有提升，且不牺牲稀疏探测下的特征级可解释性。改进在宿主模型、基线家族和稀疏度范围内一致，仅增加少量标量参数，在单个消费级GPU上数分钟即可完成。

Conclusion: RSAE通过可训练有理函数统一并超越了现有SAE家族，提供了一种轻量级、高效的升级方案，能在不牺牲可解释性的前提下持续改善SAE的重构与下游性能。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are standard tools for mechanistic interpretability, but current SAE families are constrained by fixed encoder nonlinearities such as ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK. This hard-codes a particular sparsity mechanism into the model and can distort the reconstruction-versus-sparsity trade-off. We introduce the Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE), which replaces the fixed encoder activation with a trainable rational function. Rational activations are flexible enough to uniformly approximate the activation primitives used by existing SAE families on compact domains (for TopK, the thresholded gate obtained after a separating top-k threshold is supplied), while also providing a richer function class for adapting to the observed pre-activation geometry. We realise this idea through a two-stage pipeline: an initialisation procedure that copies the pre-trained baseline SAE weights, plugs in rational coefficients obtained by the relaxed Remez exchange on synthetic data, and calibrates the scale parameters along with the rational coefficients; followed by a fine-tuning step under the standard sparsity-regularised reconstruction objective. Empirically, on residual-stream activations of three open-weight language models and across all three baseline activation families, the RSAE strictly improves on it after the fine-tuning step, both on reconstruction-side metrics and on downstream-behaviour metrics, without sacrificing feature-level interpretability under sparse probing. These gains are consistent across host language models, across baseline activation families, and across the full range of baseline sparsity we tested, while the upgrade itself adds only a handful of scalar parameters per autoencoder and runs in minutes on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [171] [Unlocking Latent Dimensions: Exploring Representations of Large-Scale X-ray Scattering Data using Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14999)
*Monika Choudhary,Xiaoya Chong,Runbo Jiang,Wiebke Koepp,Petrus H. Zwart,Damon English,Gregory M. Su,Eric Schaible,Chenhui Zhu,Mostafa Nassr,Noah P. Wamble,Kelvin Kam-Yun Li,Jonathan M. Chan,Jose Carlos Diaz,Cameron McKay,Lynn Katz,Benny Freeman,Guillaume Freychet,Yevgen Matviychuk,Eliot Gann,Daniel B. Allan,Benedikt Sochor,Frank Schluenzen,Stephan V. Roth,Ethan Crumlin,Dylan McReynolds,Tanny Chavez,Alexander Hexemer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE）的领域专用方法，利用150万张X射线散射图像学习低维潜在表征，实现离线数据集探索和实时在线分析，并集成到MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学用户设施生成的X射线散射数据速度超过传统工作流的处理能力，需要更高效的数据分析方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 训练基于注意力机制的卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE），使用150万张X射线散射图像学习低维潜在表征，并通过潜在空间探索进行离线与实时分析。

Result: 潜在空间展现出良好的聚类结构和平滑轨迹，支持可控的合成图像生成。模型无需重新训练即可在两个同步辐射设施的时间分辨薄膜形成实验中组织出可解释的潜在结构。与DINOv3（ViT-7B）通用视觉基础模型相比，领域专用训练产生了更可解释的潜在组织。

Conclusion: 该方法通过领域特定的潜在空间学习，有效加速了X射线散射数据的分析流程，并具备跨设施泛化能力，优于通用视觉基础模型。

Abstract: Scientific user facilities generate X-ray scattering data faster than traditional workflows can process them. We address this challenge across two settings, offline dataset exploration and live on-the-fly analysis. We train a domain-specific attention-based Convolutional Variational Autoencoder (C-VAE) on 1.5 million X-ray scattering images to learn low-dimensional representations capturing structural variation across diverse experimental conditions. The learned latent space reveals well-organized clusters and smooth trajectories reflecting experimental progression. It further supports controlled synthetic scattering image generation across diverse structural states. When deployed without retraining, the model organizes time-resolved film formation experiments at two synchrotron facilities into interpretable latent structures. Benchmarking against DINOv3 (ViT-7B), a general-purpose vision foundation model, demonstrates that domain-specific training yields more interpretable latent organization for scattering data. Both workflows are integrated within Latent Space Explorer, a component of the MLExchange platform, supporting interactive structural exploration across archived datasets and live experiments.

</details>


### [172] [How Should World Models Be Evaluated? A Decision-Making-Centric Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15032)
*Yang Yu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifei Sheng,Haoxiang Ren,Haoxin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了世界模型在具身决策中的评估现状，指出当前存在"声称/证据不匹配"问题，并提出以决策为中心、从L0到L7的分层评估框架，强调反事实推理与策略优化等关键能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"世界模型"概念泛化，评估指标多样且与核心声称脱节，导致论文常做出强声称但缺乏与之匹配的证据，亟需建立统一的、以决策为中心的评估标准。

Method: 通过文献综述，提出L0-L7阶梯式评估体系（从视觉合理性到策略优化效用），并设计以决策为中心的评估框架与基准协议，涵盖反事实动作保真度、闭环 rollout 有效性、策略排序一致性等关键维度。

Result: 指出现有评估多停留在L0-L3的生成物诊断层面，而L4（反事实干预测试）是首个真正的干预性测试，L5-L7才提供决策实用性的直接证据。

Conclusion: 世界模型评估应回归决策核心目标，优先检验其在干预、分布偏移和长时序rollout下的反事实推理与策略优化能力，而非单纯追求视觉质量。

Abstract: World models have rapidly become one of the central abstractions in modern AI. Yet the term now refers to several different objects: action-conditioned environment models, latent imagination models, future-video predictors, interactive neural simulators, latent predictive representations, and synthetic-data engines. Evaluation has broadened with the term. Recent papers measure video realism, perceptual similarity, instruction following, physical plausibility, policy ranking, executability, planning success, and downstream policy improvement. The result is not only metric diversity but also a recurring problem of claim/evidence mismatch: papers frequently make a stronger claim about what their model is useful for than their evaluation can actually establish.
  This paper surveys the recent literature and argues that the central question is use-dependent. When a model is presented as a world model for embodied decision-making, a more decisive issue is not whether it generates visually compelling videos, but whether it supports reliable counterfactual reasoning, policy evaluation, planning, and policy optimization under intervention, policy-induced distribution shift, and long-horizon rollout. We organize the literature using an L0--L7 ladder that ranges from visual plausibility to policy optimization utility. In our interpretation, L0--L3 are most naturally read as diagnostics of generated artifacts, L4 is often the first genuinely interventional test, and L5--L7 provide the most direct evidence of decision usefulness. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a decision-making-centric evaluation framework and a benchmark protocol that foreground counterfactual action fidelity, closed-loop rollout validity, reward/value prediction, policy-ranking agreement, optimization lift, model exploitability, and uncertainty calibration.

</details>


### [173] [Transformers Learn the Mestre-Nagao Heuristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15036)
*Pranav Venkata Konda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用两层Transformer编码器对有理椭圆曲线分类（秩0或秩1），准确率>99%，通过机械可解释性发现模型学到了Mestre-Nagao和启发式权重，以及20个MLP神经元构成的稀疏电路足以完成预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer模型能否从Frobenius迹数据中学习到数论中的深层规律，并通过机械可解释性揭示其内部工作机制。

Method: 使用两层Transformer编码器，输入128个归一化Frobenius迹，进行二分类（秩0 vs 秩1）；运用注意力分析、线性探针、激活修补、logit归因和神经元级电路分析等机械可解释性技术。

Result: 分类准确率>99%；20个MLP神经元构成的稀疏电路在AUROC上达0.992；模型学到的输入权重与Mestre-Nagao和启发式权重高度相关（Spearman r=0.997, Pearson r=0.952）；CLS嵌入编码了log L(E,1)（R²=0.962）。

Conclusion: Transformer能从数据中无监督地学习到解析数论中的重要启发式，但存在读取路径与判别电路之间的次优匹配问题。

Abstract: We train a two-layer transformer encoder to classify rational elliptic curves $E/\mathbb{Q}$ of conductor $\leq 10000$ as either rank 0 or rank 1 from the first 128 normalized Frobenius traces. We achieve >99% accuracy on both classes, and accuracy is essentially unchanged on test curves with no isogeny or quadratic-twist relative in the training set. We then apply techniques from mechanistic interpretability such as attention analysis, linear probing, activation patching, logit attribution, and neuron-level circuit analysis to reverse-engineer the algorithm the (centroid in function space) model learned. We find that a sparse circuit of 20 out of 512 layer-1 MLP neurons is sufficient for rank prediction under a linear probe with an AUROC of 0.992 at plateau, implementing a push-pull detector architecture of rank-0 and rank-1 detectors with a one-sided readout. However, we notice that the model has sub-optimal readout problems indicating a mismatch in rank-order between the readout pathway and the discriminative circuit. Critically, the learned input weights of the top discriminating neuron match the Mestre-Nagao sum heuristic weights $\log(p)/(p\cdot \log{B})$ with a Spearman coefficient $r = 0.997$ and Pearson coefficient $r = 0.952$: the model has learnt a result from analytic number theory from the Frobenius trace data alone. We additionally find that all 50 independently trained models concentrate CLS attention on prime positions at 2-50$\times$ the rate of composite positions. The CLS embedding encodes $\log{L(E,1)}$ with $R^2 = 0.962\pm 0.011$ across the 50 models (after controlling for the conductor). Activation patching analysis reveals that attention weights are dissociated from causal information flow. Additionally, the 50 solutions from training are near-identical in function space (with pairwise agreement $>$98.8%) despite large weight space barriers.

</details>


### [174] [Temporal Difference Learning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15048)
*Qizhen Ying,Yangchen Pan,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Junfeng Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种时间差分（TD）目标函数，通过强化学习中的策略评估方法，惩罚扩散模型去噪轨迹上的不一致性，从而提升生成质量，尤其在少步采样场景下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型训练目标只关注单个时间步的局部去噪，缺乏跨时间步的一致性约束，导致少步采样时性能下降。

Method: 将扩散过程重新建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，把去噪视为强化学习中的策略评估问题，推导出适用于离散和连续时间扩散的统一TD目标，并引入基于样本的稳定训练重加权方法。

Result: 使用TD训练显著提升了FID指标，尤其在采样步数较少时优势更明显。

Conclusion: TD方法可作为通用插件，强制跨时间步一致性，提升多种扩散生成模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models are typically trained with objectives that focus on local denoising targets at individual time steps (or adjacent pairs), which do not enforce consistency between predictions along the denoising trajectory. This lack of cross-time consistency can degrade performance, especially for few-step samplers. We introduce a temporal difference (TD) objective that penalizes inconsistency of the model's multi-step progress along the denoising path. By reformulating the diffusion process as a Markov reward process and casting denoising as a policy evaluation problem in reinforcement learning, we derive a unified TD approach that applies to both discrete- and continuous-time diffusion formulations. We further propose a principled sample-based reweighting method that stabilizes training. Empirically, we show that using our TD training can significantly improve sample quality measured by FID, with stronger advantages when the number of sampling steps is small, highlighting its practical utility under low-computation-budget scenarios. We provide ablation studies to justify our design choices, including pairwise loss reweighting, regularization weight, and one-step stride. Overall, our TD approach can be a general drop-in that enforces cross-time consistency and improves generation quality across different diffusion generative models.

</details>


### [175] [Physics-conforming Latent Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15053)
*Matthias Chung,Yutong Bu,Deepanshu Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Physics-conforming Latent Twins框架，通过约束潜空间动力学满足物理守恒律、不变量和耗散结构，实现物理一致的潜变量代理求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理模型虽能准确插值训练轨迹，但无法保证时间依赖问题中的守恒律、不变量和耗散结构等物理约束，导致预测结果缺乏物理意义。

Method: 在Latent Twin框架基础上联合学习编码器、解码器和潜流映射，通过约束转移方法将原始状态空间的物理结构转化为潜空间兼容约束，并推导了保持线性/二次不变量和耗散不等式的代数条件。

Result: 在ODE和PDE基准测试上的数值实验表明，该方法在保持代理预测准确性的同时，显著提升了约束满足度、结构保真度和长期定性行为。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效将物理结构融入潜变量动力学，证明了潜空间约束对解码后物理缺陷的控制作用，为物理一致的代理建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Surrogate models are central to scientific machine learning, where they enable fast prediction, simulation, inference, and control for complex physical systems. For time-dependent problems, however, accurate interpolation of training trajectories is not sufficient: reliable surrogates should also respect the conservation laws, invariants, admissibility conditions, and dissipative structures that give those trajectories physical meaning. We introduce Physics-conforming Latent Twins, a framework for learning latent surrogate solution operators whose dynamics satisfy selected physical principles by design. The method builds on the Latent Twin formulation by jointly learning an encoder, a decoder, and a latent flow map between arbitrary time-indexed states, while constraining the latent dynamics to preserve or dissipate prescribed structural quantities. We develop a constraint-transfer viewpoint that connects physical structure in the original state space with compatible constraints in latent space, and prove structure-preservation bounds showing how latent enforcement improves control of physical defects after decoding. We also derive algebraic conditions for latent flow maps that preserve linear and quadratic invariants or enforce dissipative inequalities. Numerical experiments on representative ODE and PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction, structural fidelity, and qualitative long-time behavior while maintaining accurate surrogate prediction.

</details>


### [176] [Size Doesn't Matter: Cosine-Scored Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15054)
*Silen Naihin,Lev Stambler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现BatchTopK稀疏自编码器使用内积评分会导致高范数token占据字典槽位，提出用余弦相似度与输入幅度的可学习混合替代，训练中模型从未选择超过半幅度依赖，且余弦编码器学到的特征更符合人类可识别概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器使用内积检测特征，但经过子层归一化后，模型实际读取的是方向信息而非范数，而内积评分仍受输入范数影响，导致高范数token不合理地占据字典槽位。

Method: 将内积评分替换为余弦相似度与输入幅度的可学习混合评分，并扩展到每个特征独立选择权重，让优化器自行决定范数使用程度。

Result: 训练中可以恢复到内积但从未发生，没有特征选择超过半幅度依赖；在匹配重建质量时，余弦编码器学到的特征更频繁地对齐人类可识别概念；损失重加权无法消除差距，证实前向评分几何是关键因素。

Conclusion: 余弦评分应成为归一化表示上字典学习的默认选择，尽管其优势并非在所有任务和深度上普遍适用。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) detect features via inner product, so a feature's activation scales with both its directional alignment and the input's norm. Under BatchTopK, high-norm tokens inflate all pre-activations simultaneously, claiming dictionary slots regardless of content alignment. This matters because sublayer normalization has already discarded the magnitude the score measures, so the encoder detects a quantity the model does not read. We replace the score with a learned blend of cosine similarity and input magnitude, letting the optimizer choose how much norm to use; a per-feature extension lets each feature decide independently. In both regimes, training is free to recover inner product but never does, with no feature ever choosing more than half-magnitude dependence. At matched reconstruction, the cosine encoder learns features that align with human-recognizable concepts far more often than standard, filling dictionary slots that inner product wastes on norm detectors. Loss reweighting that equalizes gradients barely closes the gap, confirming forward-pass score geometry as the lever. The advantage is not universal across tasks or depths, but we believe cosine scoring should be the default for dictionary learning on normalized representations.

</details>


### [177] [Machine Learning and the Random Walk Puzzle: Forecasting the CAD/USD Exchange Rate with Expanding Window Evaluation and SHAP Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15058)
*Louis Agyekum,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Kofi Acheampong,Emmanuel Boadi,Priscilla Yaa Amakye,Fafa Shalom Tchorly,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估五种机器学习模型（线性回归、随机森林、梯度提升、XGBoost、AdaBoost）在预测美元/加元汇率方面是否优于朴素随机游走基准。结果表明，仅线性回归在统计上显著优于随机游走（DM统计量3.0585，p值0.0071），随机森林在扩展窗口框架下取得最低MAPE（1.17%）。SHAP分析显示短期滞后项（lag1、lag2）和近期滚动均值主导预测，符合汇率接近随机游走的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汇率预测是金融领域的核心难题，传统观点认为汇率近似遵循随机游走，难以被系统性模型超越。本研究旨在检验机器学习模型是否能在美元/加元汇率预测中打破随机游走基准，为汇率可预测性提供新证据。

Method: 使用加拿大银行2017年1月至2026年5月的日度数据重采样为113个月度观测值，采用五种ML模型（线性回归、随机森林、梯度提升、XGBoost、AdaBoost）与随机游走及ETS模型对比。使用扩展窗口框架确保严格的样本外完整性，采用Diebold-Mariano检验评估预测精度差异，并进行结构断点检测和SHAP解释性分析。

Result: 线性回归是唯一在统计上显著优于随机游走的模型（DM=3.0585, p=0.0071）。随机森林在扩展窗口下取得最低MAPE（1.17%）。其余ML集成模型与随机游走差异不显著。检测到四个结构断点，分别对应2018年中美贸易战升级、2020年COVID经济复苏、2022年加拿大央行加息峰值和2024年降息周期开始。

Conclusion: 朴素随机游走仍是汇率预测的强硬基准，线性回归能提供统计上显著的改进，但ML集成模型未展示出实质性优势。汇率预测主要由短期滞后项驱动，印证了汇率的近随机游走行为特征。结构断点检测揭示了重大宏观经济事件对汇率动态的影响。

Abstract: This study examines whether machine learning (ML) models can outperform the naive random walk benchmark in forecasting the monthly USD/CAD exchange rate. Using daily data from the Bank of Canada spanning January 2017 to May 2026, resampled into 113 monthly observations, five ML models are evaluated: linear regression, random forest, gradient boosting, XGBoost, and AdaBoost. These models are benchmarked against the naive random walk model and exponential smoothing with Holt-Winters seasonality (ETS). All models are evaluated using an expanding-window framework to maintain strict out-of-sample integrity, and forecast-accuracy differences are assessed using the Diebold-Mariano (DM) test. Structural break detection identifies four significant breakpoints in the series, corresponding to the escalation of the US-China trade war in 2018, the COVID-19 economic recovery in 2020, the peak of the Bank of Canada rate-hiking cycle in 2022, and the start of the Bank of Canada rate-cutting cycle in 2024. SHAP, or Shapley Additive Explanations, analysis is applied to interpret the drivers of the best-performing ML model. The results show that the naive random walk model remains a formidable benchmark. Linear regression is the only model that statistically outperforms the naive random walk model, with a DM statistic of 3.0585 and a p value of 0.0071, whereas the ML ensemble models show only marginal differences. Random Forest with an expanding-window framework achieves the lowest MAPE of 1.17 percent among all models except the random walk. SHAP analysis confirms that short-term lags, particularly lag1 and lag2, and recent rolling means dominate predictions, consistent with the near-random-walk behavior of exchange rates.

</details>


### [178] [Phase-Localized Curation Does Not Help: A Negative Result on Per-Phase Metric Selection for Demonstration Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15064)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了“按阶段进行演示筛选”这一假设，发现在接触丰富的LIBERO任务中，分阶段筛选不仅不是最优策略，甚至比全局单一指标更差，并揭示了信号稀释和跨任务不可迁移的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示操作具有时间阶段结构，自然假设是应分阶段应用筛选指标而非全局统一，但先前工作表明全局最优检测指标未必是最优筛选指标，因此需要检验分阶段筛选是否更优。

Method: 在LIBERO拾放任务中引入“过早释放”结构缺陷，对比分阶段筛选、统一指标应用和强全局指标三种策略，使用五个随机种子重复实验，并分析失败机制。

Result: 分阶段筛选从未成为最优策略，在三个任务中的两个上表现最差（任务1：86.0 vs 全局92.0；任务3：22.7 vs 统一48.0）。失败原因是：缺陷信号集中在某阶段时，跨阶段排名聚合会稀释该信号；且分阶段指标选择无法跨任务迁移。

Conclusion: 实践者应优先寻找单一的、对缺陷信息敏感的全局指标，而非按阶段分解筛选流程。研究发布了完整流程和所有指标实现。

Abstract: Manipulation demonstrations have temporal phase structure, and a natural hypothesis is that demonstration-curation metrics should be applied within phases rather than globally. The idea is to segment each trajectory into phases, score each phase with the metric that is locally most informative, and then aggregate. This follows directly from prior work showing that a single global metric can be the best detector of a defect and yet the worst curator of the resulting policy. We test the per-phase hypothesis on three contact-rich LIBERO pick-and-place tasks with a controlled early-release structural defect, comparing phase-gated curation against the same metrics applied uniformly and against a strong single global metric. Across all three tasks and five random seeds per condition, phase-gated curation is never the best curation strategy, and it is the worst of the three on two of the three tasks (Task 1: 86.0 vs. 92.0 for global; Task 3: 22.7 vs. 48.0 for uniform). We trace the failure to a concrete mechanism. When the defect signal is concentrated in a single phase, rank-aggregating across phases dilutes that signal with uninformative scores from defect-free phases, selecting a worse demonstration subset than simply applying the defect-informative metric everywhere. We further show that the per-phase metric selection does not transfer across tasks, since no phase shares a winning metric between any two tasks, so the selection cannot be reused and must be re-derived per task from a noisy sweep. These results bound a plausible and previously untested method, and they argue that practitioners should prefer identifying a single defect-informative metric over decomposing curation by phase. We release the full pipeline, all metric implementations, and per-seed results.

</details>


### [179] [TriAdReview: Triangular Adversarial Review Architecture for Multi-Model Technical Document Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15074)
*Zhiqiang Zhou,Junliang Dai,Xu Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TriAdReview三角对抗评审架构，通过两个独立评审模型和一个三角裁决机制迭代改进生成器输出。在75个实验中，三模型配置相比单模型基线整体提升10.1%，但需求分析任务出现7.5%的性能下降，揭示了对抗评审架构在不同任务类型中的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在技术文档生成中常存在过度工程、安全盲区和覆盖不全等问题，单模型输出质量有限，需要多模型协作评审机制来提升生成质量。

Method: 提出TriAdReview三角对抗评审架构，包含工程视角和边界视角两个独立评审模型，以及一个三角裁决机制，对生成器输出进行迭代改进。在五项基准任务上使用三种配置（单模型、双模型、三模型）进行实验评估。

Result: 三模型配置相比单模型基线整体提升10.1%（26.2 vs 23.8/50分），安全审计提升27.6%，代码生成提升20.8%，架构设计提升15.6%。但需求分析任务出现7.5%的性能下降。

Conclusion: 首次实证刻画了多模型对抗评审在哪些任务中有帮助、哪些任务中有害，发现对抗评审架构存在偏向简化的结构性偏见，对完整性导向的任务不利。评审提示适配可部分缓解该问题，对协作式AI系统设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for technical document generation, yet single-model outputs often suffer from over-engineering, security blind spots, and incomplete coverage. We propose TriAdReview, a triangular adversarial review architecture that employs two independent reviewer models (engineering and boundary perspectives) and a triangular judging mechanism to iteratively improve a generator model's output. We evaluate TriAdReview across five benchmark tasks - architecture design, code generation, proposal review, security audit, and requirements analysis - using three configurations: single model (baseline), dual model (single review), and triple model (full system). Results across 75 experiments (n=5 per cell) show that the triple model configuration achieves a 10.1% overall improvement over the single model baseline (26.2 vs. 23.8 out of 50; p<0.05, paired t-test), with particularly strong gains on security audit (+27.6%), code generation (+20.8%), and architecture design (+15.6%). A second scorer (mimo-v2.5-pro) confirms the direction with a smaller effect (+2.7%), suggesting moderate inter-rater agreement. However, the system shows a -7.5% degradation on requirements analysis, revealing that adversarial review architectures have a structural bias toward simplification that is counterproductive for completeness-oriented tasks. We analyze this boundary condition through a task-type framework and demonstrate that reviewer prompt adaptation partially mitigates the issue. Our findings provide the first empirical characterization of when multi-model adversarial review helps versus harms, with implications for the design of collaborative AI systems.

</details>


### [180] [An Integrable Token Mixing Layer from the Generalized Yang Baxter Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15085)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: YB Mixer 是一种基于自由费米子和 Yang-Baxter 结构的序列 token 混合层，通过正交映射和可交换转移矩阵实现稳定、顺序无关且适应可变预算的序列处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用可积系统中的局部代数约束保证全局计算稳定性，并实现顺序无关推理，以适应不同的计算预算。

Method: 基于 Ising 交换代数构建自由费米子结构，生成精确保范的正交映射；通过谱循环生成器保持正交性和可交换性；利用可交换转移矩阵实现无顺序推理。

Result: 得到了一个高度稳定且数学基础扎实的序列处理架构，具备正交保范、顺序无关和长度泛化能力。

Conclusion: YB Mixer 为序列处理提供了一种数学上严谨且稳定的新方法，具有良好的泛化和适应能力。

Abstract: The YB Mixer is a sequence token mixing layer derived from free fermion and generalized Yang Baxter structures. It applies a core principle from integrable systems where a local algebraic constraint guarantees global computational stability. By using the Ising exchange algebra the mixer creates a free fermionic structure that acts as an exactly norm preserving orthogonal map. This algebra also produces commuting transfer matrices which allow inference to be order free and adaptable to any variable budget. To ensure the model can generalize to longer sequence lengths it uses a spectral circulant generator. This generator maintains the crucial orthogonal and commuting properties of the system. The result is a highly stable and mathematically grounded architecture for sequence processing.

</details>


### [181] [High-Dimensional Random Projection for Activation Steering in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15092)
*Minh-Hieu Pham,Bach Do,Laziz Abdullaev,Tan Minh Nguyen,Khoat Than*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HiDRA方法，通过随机投影到高维空间进行激活操控，突破了传统均值差异方法的局限性，在不增加计算开销的情况下实现了更强的LLM行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于均值差异的激活操控方法只能捕捉类别激活之间的均值差异，无法恢复在叠加假设下非线性特征子空间中自然存在的判别信号，存在根本性局限。

Method: 提出HiDRA（高维随机投影激活操控），一种无需训练的方法，通过将激活值投影到高维空间后再进行激活加法操作，能够证明性地捕捉到线性方法无法触及的更优判别结构。

Result: 在多种LLM系列和基准测试中，HiDRA持续优于基线方法，在无明显计算开销的前提下实现了更强的行为控制效果。

Conclusion: HiDRA可以有效替代现有的激活操控方法，以极低的成本提升LLM行为控制能力。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a key methodology for controlling the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Existing difference-in-means based methods, however, are fundamentally limited: they capture only mean differences between class activations and fail to recover discriminative signals that naturally exist in the nonlinear feature subspace under the superposition hypothesis. Motivated by that, we propose High-Dimensional Random-projection for Activation Steering (HiDRA), a training-free approach that integrates seamlessly with existing activation steering methods. By performing activation addition in the projected high-dimensional space, HiDRA can provably capture a better discriminative structure beyond the reach of linear methods. Experiments across diverse LLM families and benchmarks demonstrate that HiDRA consistently outperforms baseline counterparts, achieving stronger behavioral control without significant computational overhead.

</details>


### [182] [Diversity-Driven Offline Multi-Objective Optimization via Nested Pareto Set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15115)
*Yiyi Zhu,Yaolin Wen,Xiang Xia,Xin An,Hanyi Si,Xiang Shu,Yangde Fu,Liang Dou,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DOMOO方法，通过累积风险控制、嵌套Pareto集学习和多样性驱动选择策略，解决离线多目标优化中的分布外问题和解集多样性不足的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多目标优化中存在分布外（OOD）问题，导致代理模型对未见设计不准确，优化器可能选择不在真实Pareto前沿上的解，且偏向极端区域，因此需要一种能生成多样且高质量解集的方法。

Method: 1) 累积风险控制模块：估计候选解的潜在风险，缓解训练数据与生成解之间的OOD问题；2) 嵌套Pareto集学习（PSL）策略：联合学习偏好和PSL参数并优化，适应多样化的Pareto前沿几何形状；3) 多样性驱动选择策略：提出IGD_offline指标，综合考虑多样性和收敛性，提取代表性且分布均匀的最终解集。

Result: 在合成和现实世界基准测试中，DOMOO在收敛性和多样性方面均取得了最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: DOMOO有效解决了离线多目标优化中的OOD问题和解集多样性不足问题，在多个任务上表现优于对比方法。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has emerged as a powerful approach to solving complex optimization problems involving multiple objectives.
